\displaystyle~\left\lVert\dfrac{1}{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\mathbf{Y}_{l(j)}\mathbbm{1}\left(\max_{1\leq j\leq d}\|\mathbf{Y}_{l(j)}\|\leq\dfrac{C\sqrt{nd}}{(\log d)}\right)-\mathbb{E}\mathbf{Y}_{1(j)}\mathbbm{1}\left(\max_{1\leq j\leq d}\|\mathbf{Y}_{1(j)}\|\leq\dfrac{C\sqrt{nd}}{(\log d)}\right)\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\sqrt{\dfrac{Cd(t+t^{2}/(\log d))}{n}}

(80)

with probability at least
1-\exp(-t)
. Following the strategy of (
A.4
), we define
M^{\prime}:=\{1\leq l\leq n:\max_{1\leq j\leq d}\|\mathbf{Y}_{l(j)}\|^{2}\geq Cnd/(\log d)^{2}\}
. Then we have, using (
A.4
) for
t=\dfrac{1}{(\log d)^{2}}
that

\mathbb{E}|M^{\prime}|\leq d^{-2/3}+n^{-\epsilon/8}\,\,\text{ and }\,\,\mathbb{P}(|M^{\prime}|\geq C)\leq\exp(-Cd^{2/3}\wedge n^{\epsilon/8}),

by Chernoff bounds for Binomial random variables. As previously done in Lemmas
12
and
14
, we bound the second term as

\displaystyle\left\lVert\dfrac{1}{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\mathbf{Y}_{lj}\mathbbm{1}(\|\mathbf{Y}_{l(j)}\|\geq C\sqrt{nd}/(\log d))\right\rVert\leq

\displaystyle~\dfrac{1}{n}\cdot|M^{\prime}|\cdot\max_{1\leq l\leq n}\max_{1\leq j\leq d}\|\mathbf{Y}_{l(j)}\|\leq~\dfrac{C\sqrt{nd}}{n}

with probability at least
1-\exp(-d^{2/3}\wedge n^{\epsilon/8})-n^{-\tfrac{1}{3}}d^{-\tfrac{8+7\epsilon}{12}}+n^{-\tfrac{\epsilon}{8}}d^{-\tfrac{\epsilon}{8}}
, taking a union bound over
1\leq l\leq n
in (
A.4
) with
t=1
in the last step. Finally by Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, we have

\displaystyle~\left\lVert\mathbb{E}\mathbf{Y}_{1(j)}\mathbbm{1}(\|\mathbf{Y}_{1(j)}\|\geq C\sqrt{nd}/(\log d))\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\sup_{\mathbf{v}\in\mathbb{S}^{d-1}}\left(\mathbb{E}S_{1(j)}^{6}\langle\mathbf{S}_{1},\mathbf{v}\rangle^{2}\right)^{1/2}\cdot\left(\mathbb{P}(\|\mathbf{Y}_{1(j)}\|\geq\dfrac{C\sqrt{nd}}{\log d}\right)^{1/2}\leq\dfrac{C}{\sqrt{n}}

using (
A.4
) with
t=\log d
in the last step. Taking
t=4(\log(1/\delta))
in (
A.4
) finishes the proof. ∎

Lemma 16
.

Let
\mathbf{X}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{X}_{n}
be
n
independent copies of a random vector
\mathbf{X}
such that
{\cal L}(\mathbf{X})\in{\cal P}_{\rm ICA}(\mathbf{A};\epsilon,M_{1},M_{2})
for
M_{1},M_{2}>0
. Let us define

\mathscr{E}=\dfrac{1}{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\mathbf{X}_{l}^{\circ 4}-\mathbb{E}(\mathbf{X}^{\circ 4})

and the matrix
\mathbf{E}=[\mathscr{E}\times_{1,2,3}\mathbf{a}_{1}\,\mathscr{E}\times_{1,2,3}\mathbf{a}_{2}\,\dots\mathscr{E}\times_{1,2,3}\mathbf{a}_{d}]
.
Then

\mathbb{P}\left(\|\mathbf{E}\|\leq C(\sqrt{t}+1)\sqrt{\dfrac{d+d^{2}\mathbbm{1}(\epsilon<4)}{n}}\right)\leq\exp(-t)+n^{-\epsilon/8}+n^{-(\epsilon-4)/12}\mathbbm{1}(\epsilon>4).

Proof of Lemma
16
.

The proof follows the same truncation strategy from Lemmas
2
,
14
and
15
. Note that

\mathscr{E}\times_{1,2,3}\mathbf{a}_{j}=\mathbf{A}\left(\dfrac{1}{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}S_{lj}^{3}\mathbf{S}_{l}-\mathbb{E}S_{j}^{3}\mathbf{S}\right).

Writing

\mathbf{Y}=(S_{1}^{3}\,S_{2}^{3}\,\dots\,S_{d}^{3})

and letting
\mathbf{Y}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{Y}_{n}
denote independent copies of
\mathbf{Y}
, we have

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{E}\|=

\displaystyle~\left\lVert\dfrac{1}{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\mathbf{S}_{l}\mathbf{Y}_{l}^{\top}-\mathbb{E}\mathbf{S}\mathbf{Y}^{\top}\right\rVert.

Note that

\|\mathbf{S}\mathbf{Y}^{\top}\|^{2}=\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{d}S_{i}^{2}\sum_{j=1}^{d}S_{j}^{6}
,
so that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\|\mathbf{S}\mathbf{Y}^{\top}\|\geq t\right)\leq\dfrac{\mathbb{E}\left(\|\mathbf{S}\mathbf{Y}^{\top}\|^{2}\right)^{(8+\epsilon)/8}}{t^{(8+\epsilon)/4}}\leq\dfrac{d^{(8+\epsilon)/4}}{t^{(8+\epsilon)/4}}

by Theorem 1 of
Latała 1997
. Thus,

\mathbb{P}(\max\{\|\mathbf{S}\mathbf{Y}^{\top}\|\}\leq C(nd^{2}t)^{1/2})\leq\dfrac{1}{n^{\varepsilon/8}t^{(8+\epsilon)/8}}.

On the other hand if
\epsilon\geq 4
, we have

\mathbb{P}\left(\sum(S_{i}^{6}-\mathbb{E}S_{i}^{6})\geq t\right)\leq\dfrac{d^{(8+\varepsilon)/12}}{t^{(8+\varepsilon)/6}}

by Corollary 2 of
Latała 1997
, which implies that

\mathbb{P}(\max\{\|\mathbf{Y}\|\}\leq C(ndt)^{1/4})\leq\dfrac{1}{n^{(\epsilon-4)/12}t^{(8+\epsilon)/12}}.

A similar bound over
\|\mathbf{S}\|
holds and this implies,

\mathbb{P}(\max\{\|\mathbf{S}\mathbf{Y}^{\top}\|\}\leq C(ndt)^{1/2})\leq\dfrac{1}{n^{(\epsilon-4)/12}t^{(8+\epsilon)/12}}.

Next, as done in the previous lemmas, we write

\displaystyle\left\lVert\dfrac{1}{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\mathbf{S}_{l}\mathbf{Y}_{l}^{\top}-\mathbb{E}\mathbf{S}\mathbf{Y}^{\top}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\left\lVert\dfrac{1}{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\mathbf{S}_{l}\mathbf{Y}_{l}^{\top}\mathbbm{1}(\max\{\|\mathbf{S}_{l}\mathbf{Y}_{l}^{\top}\|\}\leq\eta)-\mathbb{E}\mathbf{S}\mathbf{Y}^{\top}\mathbbm{1}(\max\{\|\mathbf{S}_{l}\mathbf{Y}_{l}^{\top}\|\}\leq\eta)\right\rVert

\displaystyle+\left\lVert\dfrac{1}{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\mathbf{S}_{l}\mathbf{Y}_{l}^{\top}\mathbbm{1}(\max\{\|\mathbf{S}_{l}\mathbf{Y}_{l}^{\top}\|\}\geq\eta)\right\rVert

\displaystyle+\left\lVert\mathbb{E}\mathbf{S}_{l}\mathbf{Y}_{l}^{\top}\mathbbm{1}(\max\{\|\mathbf{S}_{l}\mathbf{Y}_{l}^{\top}\|\}\geq\eta)\right\rVert.

Note that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}(\mathbf{S}\mathbf{Y}^{\top})(\mathbf{S}\mathbf{Y}^{\top})^{\top}=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left((\sum S_{j}^{6})\mathbf{S}\mathbf{S}^{\top}\right)={\rm diag}\left(\mathbb{E}S_{1}^{8}+\sum_{j\neq 1}S_{j}^{6}\,\dots\,\mathbb{E}S_{d}^{8}+\sum_{j\neq d}S_{j}^{6}\right).

By a similar calculation on
\mathbb{E}(\mathbf{S}\mathbf{Y}^{\top})^{\top}(\mathbf{S}\mathbf{Y}^{\top})
, it follows that

\displaystyle\max\left\{\left\lVert\mathbb{E}(\mathbf{S}\mathbf{Y}^{\top})(\mathbf{S}\mathbf{Y}^{\top})^{\top}\right\rVert,\left\lVert\mathbb{E}(\mathbf{S}\mathbf{Y}^{\top})^{\top}(\mathbf{S}\mathbf{Y}^{\top})\right\rVert\right\}\leq Cd.

Thus by matrix Bernstein inequality,

\displaystyle\left\lVert\dfrac{1}{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\mathbf{S}_{l}\mathbf{Y}_{l}^{\top}\mathbbm{1}(\max\{\|\mathbf{S}_{l}\mathbf{Y}_{l}^{\top}\|\}\leq\eta)-\mathbb{E}\mathbf{S}\mathbf{Y}^{\top}\mathbbm{1}(\max\{\|\mathbf{S}_{l}\mathbf{Y}_{l}^{\top}\|\}\leq\eta)\right\rVert\leq\sqrt{\dfrac{Cdt}{n}}+\dfrac{Ct\eta}{n}

with probability at least
1-\exp(-t)
. For the second term, as before we define

\widetilde{M}:=\{1\leq l\leq n:\max\{\|\mathbf{S}_{l}\mathbf{Y}_{l}^{\top}\|\}\geq\eta\}.

It follows that

\mathbb{E}|\widetilde{M}|\leq n^{-\epsilon/8}\text{ and }\mathbb{P}(|\widetilde{M}|\geq C)\leq\exp(-n^{\epsilon/8})\text{ if }\begin{cases}\eta=C\sqrt{nd^{2}}\text{ and }\epsilon>0\\
\eta=C\sqrt{nd}\text{ and }\epsilon\geq 4.\end{cases}

by Chernoff bounds. Hence the second term can be bounded by

\displaystyle\left\lVert\dfrac{1}{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\mathbf{S}_{l}\mathbf{Y}_{l}^{\top}\mathbbm{1}(\max\{\|\mathbf{S}_{l}\mathbf{Y}_{l}^{\top}\|\}\geq C\eta\right\rVert\leq\dfrac{1}{n}\cdot|\widetilde{M}|\cdot\max_{1\leq l\leq n}\max\{\|\mathbf{S}_{l}\mathbf{Y}_{l}^{\top}\|\}\leq\dfrac{C\eta}{n}

with probability at least
1-n^{-\epsilon/8}-n^{-(\epsilon-4)/12}\mathbbm{1}(\epsilon\geq 4)
. The third term can be bounded by Cauchy-Schwarz inequality as done in Lemmas
14
and
15
.
∎

Lemma 17
.

Let
\pi
be the permutation from Theorem
3.3
. We define the
d\times(d-1)
matrix
\mathbf{A}_{j,\perp}=[\mathbf{a}_{1}\,\mathbf{a}_{2}\,\dots\,\mathbf{a}_{j-1}\,\mathbf{a}_{j+1}\,\dots\,\mathbf{a}_{d}]
, i.e., with all the columns of
\mathbf{A}
except
\mathbf{a}_{j}
. We then have

\langle\widehat{\mathbf{a}}_{\pi(j)},\mathbf{a}_{j}\rangle^{2}-1+\dfrac{1}{\kappa_{j}^{2}}\cdot(\mathbf{A}_{j,\perp}^{\top}\mathscr{E}\times_{2,3,4}\mathbf{a}_{j})^{\top}(\mathbf{A}_{j,\perp}^{\top}\mathscr{E}\times_{2,3,4}\mathbf{a}_{j})\leq\left(\dfrac{Cd(\log d)}{n}\right)^{3/2}

and

\sup_{\mathbf{u}\in\mathbb{S}^{d-1},\mathbf{u}\perp\mathbf{a}_{j}}\left\lvert\langle\mathbf{u},\widehat{\mathbf{a}}_{\pi(j)}\rangle-\dfrac{\kappa_{j}+\mathscr{E}\times_{1,2,3,4}\mathbf{a}_{j}}{\kappa_{j}^{2}}\left(\mathbf{u}^{\top}\mathscr{E}\times_{2,3,4}\mathbf{a}_{j}+3\mathbf{u}^{\top}(\mathscr{E}\times_{3,4}\mathbf{a}_{j})^{2}\mathbf{a}_{j}\right)\right\rvert\leq\dfrac{Cd^{2}(\log d)^{2}}{n^{3/2}}.

for all
1\leq j\leq d
, with probability at least
1-d^{-3}-n^{-\epsilon/8}
.

Proof of Lemma
17
.

We assume without loss of generality that
\pi=Id
. Note that we use the power iteration estimator
\widehat{\mathbf{a}}_{j}:=\widehat{\mathbf{a}}_{j,[T+1]}
for some
T>C\log d
. Thus,
\check{\mathbf{a}}_{j}:=\widehat{\mathbf{a}}_{j,[T]}
satisfy

\sin\angle\left(\check{\mathbf{a}}_{j},\mathbf{a}_{j}\right)\leq\sqrt{\dfrac{Cd(\log d)}{n}}

by Theorem
3.3
. In addition to the error quantities used earlier, we also define

\varepsilon_{0}=\max_{j}\mathscr{E}\times_{1,2,3,4}\mathbf{a}_{j}=\max_{j}\left\lvert\dfrac{1}{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}(S_{lj}^{4}-\mathbb{E}S_{1j}^{4})\right\rvert.

By (
91
), we have that

\mathbb{P}\left(\varepsilon_{0}\geq\sqrt{\dfrac{Cd}{n(\log d)}}\right)\leq\dfrac{(\log d)^{1+\epsilon/8}}{d^{\epsilon/8}}.

Let us define the event

{\cal A}\equiv\left(\varepsilon_{0}\leq\sqrt{\dfrac{Cd}{n(\log d)}},\,\,\varepsilon_{1}\leq\sqrt{\dfrac{Cd(\log d)}{n}},\,\,\varepsilon_{2}\leq\sqrt{\dfrac{Cd(\log d)}{n}},\,\,\|\mathscr{E}\|\leq\sqrt{\dfrac{Cd^{2}(\log d)^{2}}{n}}\right)

(81)

By Lemmas
2
,
14
and
15
, we have that the event
{\cal A}
holds with probability at least
1-d^{-3}-n^{-\epsilon/8}
.

By the definition of the power iteration estimator,
\widehat{\mathbf{a}}_{j}
is the top eigenvector of the symmetric matrix

\displaystyle\mathbf{M}:=\left((\mathscr{T}-\mathscr{M}_{0})\times_{2,3,4}\check{\mathbf{a}}_{j}\right)\left((\mathscr{T}-\mathscr{M}_{0})\times_{2,3,4}\check{\mathbf{a}}_{j}\right)^{\top}=

\displaystyle~\widehat{\kappa}_{j}^{2}\mathbf{a}_{j}\left(\mathbf{a}_{j}\right)^{\top}+\mathbf{Y}\mathbf{Y}^{\top}+\widehat{\kappa}_{j}\mathbf{a}_{j}\mathbf{Y}^{\top}+\widehat{\kappa}_{j}\mathbf{Y}\left(\mathbf{a}_{j}\right)^{\top}

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle~\widehat{\kappa}_{j}^{2}\mathbf{a}_{j}\left(\mathbf{a}_{j}\right)^{\top}+\mathbf{G}

(82)

where
\widehat{\kappa}_{j}:=\kappa_{j}(\langle\mathbf{a}_{j},\check{\mathbf{a}}_{j}\rangle)^{3}

and

\mathbf{Y}=\sum_{i\neq j}\kappa_{i}\left(\langle\mathbf{a}_{i},\,\check{\mathbf{a}}_{j}\rangle\right)^{3}\mathbf{a}_{i}+\mathscr{E}\times_{2,3,4}\check{\mathbf{a}}_{j}=:\mathbf{A}_{j,\perp}\widetilde{\bm{\kappa}}+\widehat{\mathbf{E}},

(83)

where
\widetilde{\bm{\kappa}}\in\mathbb{R}^{d-1}
has entries
\kappa_{i}\left(\langle\mathbf{a}_{i},\,\check{\mathbf{a}}_{j}\rangle\right)^{3}
, and

\widehat{\mathbf{E}}:=\mathscr{E}\times_{2,3,4}\check{\mathbf{a}}_{j}
.

By Theorem
3.3
, we obtain

\kappa_{j}\left(1-\dfrac{Cd(\log d)}{n}\right)^{3}\leq\kappa_{j}|\langle\check{\mathbf{a}}_{j},\mathbf{a}_{j}\rangle|^{3}=|\widehat{\kappa}_{j}|\leq\kappa_{\max}.

(84)

On the other hand, under the event
{\cal A}
,

\displaystyle\|\widetilde{\bm{\kappa}}\|\leq\kappa_{\max}\max\limits_{i\neq j}\left(\langle\mathbf{a}_{i},\check{\mathbf{a}}_{j}\rangle\right)^{2}\cdot\sin\angle\left(\check{\mathbf{a}}_{j},\mathbf{a}_{j}\right)\leq\left(\dfrac{Cd(\log d)}{n}\right)^{3/2}

by Theorem
3.3
. Following the steps of (
A.4
), we next have, under the event
{\cal A}
,

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\mathbf{E}}\|=\|\mathscr{E}\times_{2,3,4}\check{\mathbf{a}}_{j}\|\leq

\displaystyle~\|\mathscr{E}\times_{2,3,4}\mathbf{a}_{j}\|+C\sin\angle\left(\check{\mathbf{a}}_{j},\mathbf{a}_{j}\right)\cdot\varepsilon_{2}~+C\sin\angle\left(\check{\mathbf{a}}_{j},\mathbf{a}_{j}\right)^{2}\cdot\|\mathscr{E}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\sqrt{\dfrac{Cd(\log d)}{n}}

(85)

by (
36
) and Lemmas
2
,
14
and
15
. We then have by equations (
A.4
) and (
83
) that, under
{\cal A}
,

\max\{\|\mathbf{G}\|,\,\|\mathbf{Y}\|\}\leq\sqrt{\dfrac{Cd(\log d)}{n}}.

(86)

Using resolvent based series expansion of projection matrices, we have the following expression. See Theorem 1 from
Xia 2021
and Lemma 1 of
Koltchinskii and Lounici 2016
. We use the notation
\mathbf{P}_{j,\perp}:=\mathbf{A}_{j,\perp}\mathbf{A}_{j,\perp}^{\top}
.

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathbf{a}}_{j}\widehat{\mathbf{a}}_{j}^{\top}-\mathbf{a}_{j}\mathbf{a}_{j}^{\top}=

\displaystyle~\dfrac{1}{\widehat{\kappa}_{j}^{2}}\mathbf{P}_{j,\perp}\mathbf{G}\mathbf{a}_{j}\mathbf{a}_{j}^{\top}+\dfrac{1}{\widehat{\kappa}_{j}^{2}}\mathbf{a}_{j}\mathbf{a}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{G}\mathbf{P}_{j,\perp}

\displaystyle~+\dfrac{1}{\widehat{\kappa}_{j}^{4}}\left(\mathbf{a}_{j}\mathbf{a}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{G}\mathbf{P}_{j,\perp}\mathbf{G}\mathbf{P}_{j,\perp}+\mathbf{P}_{j,\perp}\mathbf{G}\mathbf{a}_{j}\mathbf{a}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{G}\mathbf{P}_{j,\perp}+\mathbf{P}_{j,\perp}\mathbf{G}\mathbf{P}_{j,\perp}\mathbf{G}\mathbf{a}_{j}\mathbf{a}_{j}^{\top}\right)

\displaystyle~-\dfrac{1}{\widehat{\kappa}_{j}^{4}}\left(\mathbf{P}_{j,\perp}\mathbf{G}\mathbf{a}_{j}\mathbf{a}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{G}\mathbf{a}_{j}\mathbf{a}_{j}^{\top}+\mathbf{a}_{j}\mathbf{a}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{G}\mathbf{P}_{j,\perp}\mathbf{G}\mathbf{a}_{j}\mathbf{a}_{j}^{\top}+\mathbf{a}_{j}\mathbf{a}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{G}\mathbf{a}_{j}\mathbf{a}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{G}\mathbf{P}_{j,\perp}\right)

\displaystyle~+R_{3}(\mathbf{G}).

(87)

Moreover,
\|R_{3}(\mathbf{G})\|\leq\dfrac{C\|\mathbf{G}\|^{3}}{\widehat{\kappa}_{j}^{6}}\leq\left(\dfrac{Cd(\log d)}{n}\right)^{3/2}
under the event
{\cal A}
.

To derive an asymptotic expression for linear forms
\langle\mathbf{u},\widehat{\mathbf{a}}_{j}\rangle
we make two cases.

Case 1:
\mathbf{u}=\mathbf{a}_{j}
.

We pre and post multiply the matrix valued expressions in (
A.4
) by
\mathbf{a}_{j}
. Then,

\displaystyle\langle\widehat{\mathbf{a}}_{j},\mathbf{a}_{j}\rangle^{2}-1=

\displaystyle~-\dfrac{1}{\widehat{\kappa}_{j}^{4}}\cdot\mathbf{a}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{G}\mathbf{P}_{j,\perp}\mathbf{G}\mathbf{a}+\mathbf{a}_{j}^{\top}R_{3}(\mathbf{G})\mathbf{a}_{j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~-\dfrac{1}{\widehat{\kappa}_{j}^{4}}\cdot(\mathbf{A}_{j,\perp}^{\top}\mathbf{G}\mathbf{a}_{j})^{\top}(\mathbf{A}_{j,\perp}^{\top}\mathbf{G}\mathbf{a}_{j})+\mathbf{a}_{j}^{\top}R_{3}(\mathbf{G})\mathbf{a}_{j}.

By (
A.4
) and (
83
),

\displaystyle\mathbf{A}_{j,\perp}^{\top}\mathbf{G}\mathbf{a}_{j}=

\displaystyle~(\widehat{\kappa}_{j}+\mathbf{a}_{j}^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{E}})(\widetilde{\bm{\kappa}}+\mathbf{A}_{j,\perp}^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{E}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\widehat{\kappa}_{j}\mathbf{A}_{j,\perp}^{\top}\mathscr{E}\times_{2,3,4}\mathbf{a}_{j}+\widehat{\kappa}_{j}\cdot\widetilde{\bm{\kappa}}+(\mathbf{a}_{j}^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{E}})(\widetilde{\bm{\kappa}}+\mathbf{A}_{j,\perp}^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{E}})+\widehat{\kappa}_{j}\mathbf{A}_{j,\perp}^{\top}(\widehat{\mathbf{E}}-\mathscr{E}\times_{2,3,4}\mathbf{a}_{j})

and hence, under the event
{\cal A}
,

\displaystyle~\left\lVert\mathbf{A}_{j,\perp}^{\top}\mathbf{G}\mathbf{a}_{j}-\widehat{\kappa}_{j}\mathbf{A}_{j,\perp}^{\top}\mathscr{E}\times_{2,3,4}\mathbf{a}_{j}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\|\widehat{\mathbf{E}}\|^{2}+(\kappa_{\max}+\|\widehat{\mathbf{E}}\|)(\|\widetilde{\bm{\kappa}}\|+C\sin\angle\left(\check{\mathbf{a}}_{j},\mathbf{a}_{j}\right)\varepsilon_{2}+C\sin\angle\left(\check{\mathbf{a}}_{j},\mathbf{a}_{j}\right)^{2}\|\mathscr{E}\|)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\dfrac{Cd(\log d)}{n}

by (
A.4
), along with Lemmas
2
,
14
and Theorem
3.3
. This implies that under the event
{\cal A}
,

\|\mathbf{A}_{j,\perp}^{\top}\mathbf{G}\mathbf{a}_{j}\|\leq|\widehat{\kappa}_{j}|\cdot\varepsilon_{1}+\dfrac{Cd(\log d)}{n}\leq\sqrt{\dfrac{Cd(\log d)}{n}}

by (
36
), (
84
) and Theorem
3.3
. We then have, using the bounds on
\widehat{\kappa}_{j}
,
\|\mathbf{G}\|
and
\|R_{3}(\mathbf{G})\|
, that

\displaystyle~\left\lvert\langle\widehat{\mathbf{a}}_{j},\mathbf{a}_{j}\rangle^{2}-1+\dfrac{1}{\kappa_{j}^{2}}\cdot(\mathbf{A}_{j,\perp}^{\top}\mathscr{E}\times_{2,3,4}\mathbf{a}_{j})^{\top}(\mathbf{A}_{j,\perp}^{\top}\mathscr{E}\times_{2,3,4}\mathbf{a}_{j})\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\dfrac{1}{\kappa_{j}^{4}}\left\lVert\mathbf{A}_{j,\perp}^{\top}\mathbf{G}\mathbf{a}_{j}-\widehat{\kappa}_{j}\mathbf{A}_{j,\perp}^{\top}\mathscr{E}\times_{2,3,4}\mathbf{a}_{j}\right\rVert\cdot\|\mathbf{A}_{j,\perp}^{\top}\mathbf{G}\mathbf{a}_{j}\|+\mathbf{a}_{j}^{\top}R_{3}(\mathbf{G})\mathbf{a}_{j}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\dfrac{Cd(\log d)}{n}\cdot\|\mathbf{A}_{j,\perp}^{\top}\mathbf{G}\mathbf{a}_{j}\|+\|R_{3}(\mathbf{G})\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\dfrac{Cd^{3/2}(\log d)^{3/2}}{n^{3/2}}.

(88)

Case 2:
\mathbf{u}\in\mathbb{S}^{d-1},\mathbf{u}\perp\mathbf{a}_{j}
.

We pre and post multiply the matrix valued expressions in (
A.4
) by
\mathbf{u}
and
\mathbf{a}_{j}
respectively. Then,

\displaystyle\langle\mathbf{u},\widehat{\mathbf{a}}_{j}\rangle\langle\widehat{\mathbf{a}}_{j},\mathbf{a}_{j}\rangle=

\displaystyle~\dfrac{1}{\widehat{\kappa}_{j}^{2}}\mathbf{u}^{\top}\mathbf{G}\mathbf{a}_{j}+\dfrac{1}{\widehat{\kappa}_{j}^{4}}\left(\mathbf{u}^{\top}\mathbf{G}\mathbf{P}_{j,\perp}\mathbf{G}\mathbf{a}_{j}-\mathbf{u}^{\top}\mathbf{G}\mathbf{a}_{j}\mathbf{a}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{G}\mathbf{a}_{j}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\dfrac{1}{\widehat{\kappa}_{j}^{2}}\mathbf{u}^{\top}\mathbf{G}\mathbf{a}_{j}+\dfrac{1}{\widehat{\kappa}_{j}^{4}}\left(\mathbf{u}^{\top}\mathbf{G}\mathbf{A}_{j,\perp}\mathbf{A}_{j,\perp}^{\top}\mathbf{G}\mathbf{a}_{j}-\mathbf{u}^{\top}\mathbf{G}\mathbf{a}_{j}\mathbf{a}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{G}\mathbf{a}_{j}\right).

As before we use (
A.4
) and (
83
) to write

\mathbf{u}^{\top}\mathbf{G}\mathbf{a}_{j}=\mathbf{u}^{\top}\mathbf{Y}\mathbf{Y}^{\top}\mathbf{a}_{j}+\widehat{\kappa}_{j}\mathbf{u}^{\top}\mathbf{Y}=\mathbf{u}^{\top}(\mathbf{A}_{j,\perp}\widetilde{\bm{\kappa}}+\widehat{\mathbf{E}})(\mathbf{a}_{j}^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{E}}+\widehat{\kappa}_{j}).

We will write, following the steps of (
A.4
), that

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathbf{E}}=

\displaystyle~\mathscr{E}\times_{2,3,4}\check{\mathbf{a}}_{j}=~\mathscr{E}\times_{2,3,4}\mathbf{a}_{j}+3(\mathscr{E}\times_{3,4}\mathbf{a}_{j})^{2}\mathbf{a}_{j}+O\left(\varepsilon_{1}^{2}(\varepsilon_{2}+\|\mathscr{E}\|)\right).

Under the event
{\cal A}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\left\lvert\mathbf{u}^{\top}(\mathbf{A}_{j,\perp}\widetilde{\bm{\kappa}}+\widehat{\mathbf{E}})-\mathbf{u}^{\top}\mathscr{E}\times_{2,3,4}\mathbf{a}_{j}-3\mathbf{u}^{\top}(\mathscr{E}\times_{3,4}\mathbf{a}_{j})^{2}\mathbf{a}_{j}\right\rvert\leq

\displaystyle~\|\widetilde{\bm{\kappa}}\|+C\varepsilon_{1}^{2}(\varepsilon_{2}+\|\mathscr{E}\|)\leq~\dfrac{Cd^{2}(\log d)^{2}}{n^{3/2}}.

Thus, under
{\cal A}
,

\displaystyle\left\lvert\mathbf{u}^{\top}\mathbf{G}\mathbf{a}_{j}-(\kappa_{j}+\mathscr{E}\times_{1,2,3,4}\mathbf{a}_{j})\left(\mathbf{u}^{\top}\mathscr{E}\times_{2,3,4}\mathbf{a}_{j}+3\mathbf{u}^{\top}(\mathscr{E}\times_{3,4}\mathbf{a}_{j})^{2}\mathbf{a}_{j}\right)\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~(\|\widehat{\kappa}_{j}-\kappa_{j}\|+|\mathbf{a}_{j}^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{E}}-\mathscr{E}\times_{1,2,3,4}\mathbf{a}_{j}|)\|\mathbf{u}^{\top}(\mathbf{A}_{j,\perp}\widetilde{\bm{\kappa}}+\widehat{\mathbf{E}})\|+(\widehat{\kappa}_{j}+\mathbf{a}^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{E}})\cdot\dfrac{Cd^{2}(\log d)^{2}}{n^{3/2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\dfrac{Cd(\log d)\varepsilon_{2}}{n}+\dfrac{Cd^{2}(\log d)^{2}}{n^{3/2}}\leq~\dfrac{Cd^{2}(\log d)^{2}}{n^{3/2}}.

(89)

Similarly by (
86
), under the event
{\cal A}
,

\|\mathbf{u}^{\top}\mathbf{G}\mathbf{A}_{j,\perp}\|=\|\mathbf{u}^{\top}\mathbf{Y}\mathbf{Y}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{j,\perp}\|\leq\|\mathbf{Y}\|^{2}\leq\dfrac{Cd(\log d)}{n}.

Finally, under the event
{\cal A}
,

\mathbf{a}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{G}\mathbf{a}_{j}=(\mathbf{a}_{j}^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{E}})^{2}+2\widehat{\kappa}_{j}\mathbf{a}_{j}^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{E}}\leq\|\mathscr{E}\times_{1,2,3,4}\mathbf{a}_{j}\|+\dfrac{Cd(\log d)}{n}\leq\dfrac{Cd(\log d)}{n}.

Plugging in the bounds into the expression for
\langle\mathbf{u},\mathbf{a}_{j}\rangle
we thus have

\displaystyle\left\lvert\langle\mathbf{u},\widehat{\mathbf{a}}_{j}\rangle\langle\widehat{\mathbf{a}}_{j},\mathbf{a}_{j}\rangle-\dfrac{1}{\widehat{\kappa}_{j}^{2}}\mathbf{u}^{\top}\mathbf{G}\mathbf{a}_{j}\right\rvert\leq

\displaystyle~\dfrac{1}{\widehat{\kappa}_{j}^{4}}\cdot\left(\|\mathbf{u}^{\top}\mathbf{G}\mathbf{A}_{j,\perp}\|\|\mathbf{A}_{j,\perp}^{\top}\mathbf{G}\mathbf{a}_{j}\|+|\mathbf{u}^{\top}\mathbf{G}\mathbf{a}_{j}|\cdot|\mathbf{a}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{G}\mathbf{a}_{j}|\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\dfrac{2\|\mathbf{G}\|}{\kappa_{j}^{4}}\cdot\dfrac{Cd(\log d)}{n}=\left(\dfrac{Cd(\log d)}{n}\right)^{3/2}.

Finally using (
A.4
) we have

\displaystyle\left\lvert\langle\mathbf{u},\widehat{\mathbf{a}}_{j}\rangle\langle\widehat{\mathbf{a}}_{j},\mathbf{a}_{j}\rangle-\dfrac{\kappa_{j}+\mathscr{E}\times_{1,2,3,4}\mathbf{a}_{j}}{\widehat{\kappa}_{j}^{2}}\left(\mathbf{u}^{\top}\mathscr{E}\times_{2,3,4}\mathbf{a}_{j}+3\mathbf{u}^{\top}(\mathscr{E}\times_{3,4}\mathbf{a}_{j})^{2}\mathbf{a}_{j}\right)\right\rvert\leq\dfrac{Cd^{2}(\log d)^{2}}{n^{3/2}}.

Note that by Theorem
3.3
,

\langle\widehat{\mathbf{a}}_{j},\mathbf{a}_{j}\rangle\geq 1-\tfrac{Cd(\log d)}{n},

and thus, also using (
84
), we have

\left\lvert\langle\mathbf{u},\widehat{\mathbf{a}}_{j}\rangle-\dfrac{\kappa_{j}+\mathscr{E}\times_{1,2,3,4}\mathbf{a}_{j}}{\kappa_{j}^{2}}\left(\mathbf{u}^{\top}\mathscr{E}\times_{2,3,4}\mathbf{a}_{j}+3\mathbf{u}^{\top}(\mathscr{E}\times_{3,4}\mathbf{a}_{j})^{2}\mathbf{a}_{j}\right)\right\rvert\leq\dfrac{Cd^{2}(\log d)^{2}}{n^{3/2}}.

(90)

This finishes the proof.
∎

Lemma 18
.

If
\mathbf{S}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
has independent random entries
S_{j}
satisfying
\mathbb{E}S_{j}=0
,
\mathbb{E}S_{j}^{2}=1
and
\mathbb{E}|S_{j}|^{\alpha}\leq C
for some
\alpha>4
, then we have

\mathbb{P}\left(\left\lvert\|\mathbf{S}\|^{2}-d\right\rvert\geq t\right)\leq\dfrac{\max\{(16d)^{\alpha/4},d\max_{j}\mathbb{E}|2S_{j}|^{\alpha}\}}{t^{\alpha/2}}.

Proof of Lemma
18
.

Note that

\|\mathbf{S}\|^{2}-d=\sum_{j=1}^{d}Y_{j}

where
Y_{j}=S_{j}^{2}-1
are independent mean zero random variables. By symmetrization, followed by an application of Rosenthal inequalities (see Corollary 2 and Remark 2 of
Latała 1997
), we have

\mathbb{E}\left(\left\lvert\sum_{j=1}^{d}Y_{j}\right\rvert\right)^{\alpha/2}\leq 2^{\alpha}\max\left\{\left(\sum_{j}\mathbb{E}Y_{j}^{2}\right)^{\alpha/4},\,\sum_{j}\mathbb{E}|Y_{j}|^{\alpha/2}\right\}\leq 2^{\alpha}C\max\{d^{\alpha/4},d\max_{j}\mathbb{E}|S_{j}|^{\alpha}\}.

(91)

The conclusion then follows by Markov’s inequality.
∎

Lemma 19
.

For a random vector
\mathbf{S}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
whose elements are independent random variables
S_{k}
satisfying
\mathbb{E}S_{k}=0
,
\mathbb{E}S_{k}^{2}=1
and
\mathbb{E}|S_{k}|^{\alpha}\leq C
for some
\alpha,C>0
, we have

\sup_{\mathbf{u}\in\mathbb{S}^{d-1}}\mathbb{E}|\langle\mathbf{S},\mathbf{u}\rangle|\leq 2^{\alpha}\max\mathbb{E}|S_{k}|^{\alpha}\leq C.

Proof.

For any
\mathbf{u}\in\mathbb{S}^{d-1}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}|\langle\mathbf{S},\mathbf{u}\rangle|^{\alpha}=

\displaystyle~\mathbb{E}\left\lvert\sum_{k}S_{k}u_{k}\right\rvert^{\alpha}\leq C\mathbb{E}\left\lvert\sum_{k}\varepsilon_{k}S_{k}u_{k}\right\rvert^{\alpha}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~2^{\alpha}\max\left\{\left(\mathbb{E}(\sum_{k}S_{k}u_{k})^{2}\right)^{\alpha/2},\,\sum_{k=1}^{d}\mathbb{E}|S_{k}u_{k}|^{\alpha}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~2^{\alpha}\max\left\{\left(\sum_{k}u_{k}^{2}\right)^{\alpha/2},\,\sum_{k=1}^{d}\mathbb{E}|S_{k}u_{k}|^{\alpha}\right\}\leq 2^{\alpha}\max\mathbb{E}|S_{k}|^{\alpha}

In the above we have used
\varepsilon_{k}
to denote independent Rademacher random variables, and Khintchine inequalities, conditional on
S_{k}
, for the first inequality. The second inequality uses Rosenthal inequalities on the symmetrized random variables
\varepsilon_{k}S_{k}
. The last step uses moment assumptions on
S_{k}
. See Remark 2 of
Latała 1997
for more details.
∎
